([WizardingCode-io/wizardingcode-developer-crew](https://github.com/WizardingCode-io/wizardingcode-developer-crew/tree/HEAD/plugins/core/skills/wizardingcode-agentic-improvement-loop))
- **wizardingcode-agentic-knowledge**: The agentic-os team's rules for a product's knowledge as the crew's own files hold them: which engines enter and by what reading, a component built under the product's own access control, and a server that writes tested never against the client's real data. - **wizardingcode-agentic-memory**: The agentic-os team's rules for a product's memory as the crew's own files hold them: which tools enter and by what reading, a component built under the product's own access control, and a server that writes tested never against the client's real data. - **wizardingcode-agentic-proactivity-unattended**: The agentic-os team's rules for a product that acts with nobody asking as the crew's own files hold them: a trigger with a rate limit, active hours and a silent outcome, a person's approval of what sends, buys, deletes or publishes, and what is read treated as data. ([WizardingCode-io/wizardingcode-developer-crew](https://github.com/WizardingCode-io/wizardingcode-developer-crew/tree/HEAD/plugins/core/skills/wizardingcode-agentic-proactivity-unattended))
- **wizardingcode-agentic-routing-measurement**: The agentic-os team's rules for routing and its measure as the crew's own files hold them: the simplest form that does the work, the client's cases run several times, the task's outcome measured apart, and nothing of the client sent to Promptfoo's service. - **wizardingcode-agentic-skills-plugins**: The agentic-os team's rules for a skill and a plugin as the crew's own files hold them: a set read whole before it enters, the skills alone with no hook that approves by itself, a registry never installed from unread, and a deploy left to a person. - **wizardingcode-agentic-watch**: The agentic-os team's rules for keeping current as the crew's own files hold them: a novelty entering only through the sources ledger, the stack's first-party route first, and the reference assistants studied, never installed and never copied.
